import BitByBitRenderCanvas from '@site/src/components/BitByBitRenderCanvas';

# Build a drone: payload and sizes

The last parts hang under the frame: a camera on its gimbal and the battery in its straps. Then one number shows what the functions were made for.

{/* steps:start - generated from the tutorial's steps, which the capture plays in the editor; edits between these markers are overwritten */}

1. The payload hangs under the frame: a camera on its gimbal at the front, its lens in glass, and the battery in two straps.

   - **Make the function** ***gimbal***:
     - set *bracket* to **extrude**(shape **face from wire**(shape **fillet 2d**(shape **polygon wire**([**point xyz**(-4, 19, -4), **point xyz**(19.5, 19, -4), **point xyz**(19.5, -7, -4), **point xyz**(14.5, -7, -4), **point xyz**(14.5, 15, -4), **point xyz**(-4, 15, -4)]), radius 1.5), planar true), direction **vector xyz**(0, 0, 8))
     - **return** **translate**(shape **union**(shapes [**cylinder**(radius 9, height 8, center **point xyz**(0, 18, 0), direction **vector xyz**(0, 1, 0)), *bracket*, **cylinder**(radius 6, height 5, center **point xyz**(14.5, 0, 0), direction **vector xyz**(-1, 0, 0))], keep edges false), translation **vector xyz**(0, -26, 55))
   - **Make the function** ***camera***:
     - **return** **union**(shapes [**fillet edges**(shape **box**(width 24, length 20, height 18, center **point xyz**(0, -26, 55)), radius 3), **cylinder**(radius 6.5, height 9, center **point xyz**(0, -26, 63), direction **vector xyz**(0, 0, 1))], keep edges false)
   - **Make the function** ***lens***:
     - **return** **cylinder**(radius 5.2, height 1.5, center **point xyz**(0, -26, 71.5), direction **vector xyz**(0, 0, 1))
   - **Make the function** ***battery***:
     - **return** **fillet edges**(shape **box**(width 36, length 72, height 26, center **point xyz**(0, -16, -20)), radius 3)
   - **Make the function** ***straps***:
     - set *strap* to **difference**(shape **box**(width 40, length 10, height 30, center **point xyz**(0, 0, 0)), shapes [**box**(width 36.5, length 12, height 26.5, center **point xyz**(0, 0, 0))], keep edges false)
     - **return** **make compound**([**translate**(shape *strap*, translation **vector xyz**(0, -16, -42)), **translate**(shape *strap*, translation **vector xyz**(0, -16, 2))])
   - **Add a stack under the others:**
     - **draw any async no return**(entity ***gimbal***(), options *aluminium*)
     - set *polymer* to ***look***("polymer", `#2a2d31`, 0.05, 0.4, `#000000`, `#f2f2f2`, 6)
     - **draw any async no return**(entity [***camera***(), ***battery***()], options *polymer*)
     - **draw any async no return**(entity ***lens***(), options ***look***("glass", `#0c1522`, 0.1, 0.05, `#000000`, `#f2f2f2`, 4))
     - **draw any async no return**(entity ***straps***(), options *accent*)
   - **Run it** with <Ui>Run The Code</Ui>.

   <Shot id="blockly.tutorials.drone/payload" />

2. Change one number: with six arms the drone rebuilds, everything around repeats follows, and the propeller shrinks to fit between its neighbours.

   - **In** set *arms* to 4, **change** 4 **to** 6.
   - **Run it** with <Ui>Run The Code</Ui>.

   <Shot id="blockly.tutorials.drone/six-arms" />

### The finished drone
